## Document 032: 20261015【投研報告】市場快訊_通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果

通膨升溫壓抑美股表現，近期聚焦財報結果
2026 年 10 月 15 日
一、 市場動態
1. 科技股領跌，費半連 6 日收黑：美股主要指數 10 月 14 日全數下跌，道瓊工業指數下
跌 0.09%，收 51,621.48 點；S&P 500 指數下跌 1.11%，收 7,718.94 點，為 7 月 29
日以來最大單日跌幅；Nasdaq 綜合指數下跌 1.88%，收 26,692.87 點，為 7 月 23
日以來最大單日跌幅。費城半導體指數下跌 4.30%，收 11,684.38 點，連續第６個交
易日走低，為 2025 年 5 月以來最長連跌紀錄。

2. 類股非全面下挫，資金仍呈輪動現象：半導體及 AI 相關股票在 10/14 跌幅尤其明顯
，AMD 下跌 5.73%、Nvidia 下跌 3.16%、Broadcom 下跌 3.10%，AI 基礎建設相關
的 CoreWeave、Nebius、Oracle 及 Dell 跌幅更深，顯示市場對資本支出規模較大、
融資成本敏感度較高的 AI 相關企業風險偏好明顯下降。不過，S&P 500 的 11 大類
股中有 8 類下跌，資訊科技及通訊服務為主要拖累來源，但醫療保健、必需消費與能
源類股仍逆勢上漲。
3. 美國 10 年期公債殖利率盤中急升後回落：美國 10 年期公債殖利率 10/14 由 5.2%左
右快速升至約 5.33%，逼近近期高點；然而，隨後市場消化 Fed 官員較審慎的政策訊
號，殖利率自高檔回落，終場約收於 5.19%，較前一交易日 5.21%小幅下降約 2 個基
點。
二、 下跌原因分析
1. 美 9 月通膨高於預期，Fed 再升息風險重新升高：美國 10/14 公布 9 月 CPI 月增 0.6%

、年增率也由 8 月的 3.4%上揚至 3.7%；核心 CPI 月增 0.3%、年增率由 8 月的 2.4%
升至 2.6%。其中汽油價格單月大漲 9.1%，貢獻整體 CPI 月增幅超過一半；住宅成本
亦月增 0.4%，顯示物價壓力仍具黏性。此數據公布後，市場重新提高 Fed 進一步升
息的預期，CME FedWatch 所反映的 10 月升息 1 碼機率一度由前一日約 22%提高至
37%。雖然截至 10/15 台北時間早上 7 點左右該機率回降至 29.3%，這也提醒投資人
Fed 的升息路徑仍存在不確定性。
2. 高利率同時壓縮科技股估值與提高 AI 融資成本：科技及半導體公司未來獲利成長占評
價的比重較高，因此對折現率變化相對敏感。當長天期美債殖利率維持 5%以上，市
場願意支付的本益比自然受到限制，即使企業獲利仍維持成長，股價也可能因評價倍
數下降而出現修正。另一方面，本輪 AI 投資需要大量資料中心、伺服器、電力與網路
基礎設施，高利率亦直接增加企業融資成本，反映信用市場對高度依賴外部資金的 AI

基礎建設公司要求更高風險溢酬。

策，應審慎衡量本身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元
大銀行所有，非經本行同意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕
不另行通知。
3. AI 投資焦點由「支出規模」轉向「投資報酬率」:近期市場對 AI 的疑慮逐漸由「需求
是否成長」，轉向「龐大的資本支出能否轉化為足夠營收、獲利與自由現金流」。
Broadcom 執行長陳福陽近期亦指出，AI 未來發展的重要限制可能逐漸由晶片或記憶
體供應轉向資本本身。我們認為，這並不代表 AI 資本支出週期即將結束，而是市場已
由第一階段的「資本支出愈大、成長想像愈高」，逐步進入更重視資本效率與投資報酬
率的第二階段。未來財務結構、現金流與 AI 商業化能力，將成為不同企業評價分化的
重要因素。
4. 中國記憶體競爭疑慮加重半導體短線壓力：中國長鑫存儲（CXMT）10/10 傳出記憶

體技術取得進展，引發市場對未來 DRAM 供給增加及價格競爭的疑慮，記憶體相關類
股率先承壓，並進一步影響整體半導體市場情緒。惟 DRAM 與 GPU、客製化 ASIC、
先進晶圓製程及先進封裝的產業競爭與需求結構並不相同。我們認為，儘管記憶體競
爭升溫值得關注，但目前尚不足以延伸為整體 AI 半導體需求同步轉弱。
5. 高檔籌碼加速獲利了結：AI 與半導體仍是 2026 年美股表現最強勢的投資主題之一，
部分科技股今年累計漲幅已相當可觀。當通膨、殖利率及 AI 投資報酬疑慮同時升高，
先前漲幅較大、投資人部位較集中的族群自然成為優先調節對象。因此，本波費半快
速修正除了總經與基本面疑慮外，亦包含籌碼降溫及風險部位收縮因素，使短期價格
波動幅度可能高於企業基本面實際變化。
三、市場看法
1. 類股輪動明顯，尚非全面看空美股：科技與 AI 相關股票 10 月 14 日明顯承壓，但醫
療保健、必需消費與能源類股仍逆勢上漲，道瓊工業指數僅下跌 0.09%。此外，S&P
500 成分股下跌家數與上漲家數之比約為 1.1：1；相較於指數下跌 1.11%，市場廣度

並未同步大幅惡化。這反映資金主要由高評價且前期漲幅較大的科技與 AI 族群，轉向
現金流較穩定的防禦型產業。現階段市場特徵較接近類股輪動與風格切換，而非無差
別的全面風險撤離，亦尚未看到美股企業獲利或經濟基本面全面反轉的明確訊號。
2. 美股科技股財報前風險降載，Q3 仍存在重新定價空間：近期科技股修正，費城半導
體指數連續６個交易日下跌，也可能部分反映第三季財報季正式展開前，投資人在股
價先前漲幅較大、評價偏高及利率環境不確定下，提前降低風險曝險。真正決定後續
市場方向的關鍵，仍在於實際企業獲利能否支持目前的高成長預期。根據 Bloomberg
季度獲利成長追蹤資料，今年 Q1 與 Q2 財報季期間的 EPS 成長率經驗顯示，隨實際
財報陸續公布，企業獲利成長曾明顯優於財報季開始前的市場預期。儘管這並不保證
Q3 將複製前兩季的上修幅度，但目前市場因財報不確定性所反映的風險折價仍存在
被實際財報重新驗證的可能。若大型科技及半導體公司後續營收、獲利與 AI 需求指引
實際公布結果優於預期，近期風險溢價可望部分收斂；反之，若獲利成長無法跟上目

前評價，市場整理時間則可能進一步延長。

不另行通知。
3. 費半修正幅度大，但 AI 需求尚未出現全面反轉：ASML 在 10/14 所公布的最新第三
季財報反而顯示 AI 相關設備需求仍相當強勁，該公司表示 2027 與 2028 年規劃產能
可能仍無法完全滿足客戶需求，部分客戶亦已表達對 2029 及 2030 年設備需求的興
趣。儘管費城半導體指數近期市場情緒轉弱，但由 ASML 的財報表現來看，目前仍未
看到大型 AI 客戶全面取消晶片訂單、主要雲端服務商同步削減 AI 資本支出，或先進
製程需求快速降溫等結構性訊號。
4. 台積電營收顯示 AI 需求暢旺，留意 10/15 法說會內容：台積電 10/8 公布的第三季營

收達新台幣 1.49 兆元、年增約 50%，創歷史新高，並高於市場原先預估的 1.46 兆元
；9 月單月營收年增 54.6%。強勁的營收表現仍反映 AI 與先進製程需求具有支撐。目
前市場因此呈現「企業營收仍快速成長，但股票評價下降」的狀況。高殖利率環境下
，EPS 上升與本益比下降可以同時發生，後續股價方向將取決於企業獲利上修幅度是
否足以抵銷評價收縮。該公司將於 10/15 下午舉行第三季法人說明會。市場重點將集
中於第四季展望、AI 與 HPC 需求、先進製程、先進封裝及未來資本支出規劃。公司
對 AI 需求與產能投資的最新判斷，將成為判斷近期費半修正主要屬於評價調整，或產
業基本面是否開始出現變化的重要依據。
5. AI 與半導體進入基本面分化階段：我們認為，在高利率環境下，高度仰賴外部融資、
自由現金流尚未成熟的 AI 基礎建設業者，受到的資金成本壓力明顯高於具穩定現金流
的大型科技與雲端服務商。同樣地，記憶體、GPU、ASIC、先進製程及先進封裝各有
不同供需結構。我們預期下一階段 AI 行情將更加重視訂單能見度、產品競爭力、毛利

率、自由現金流及 AI 營收實際兌現，產業內部強弱分化程度可能進一步提高。
6. Fed 路徑仍存不確定性，惟企業獲利可望提供股市支撐：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使市場重
新評估 Fed 後續升息時點與幅度，美國利率政策仍存在不確定性，長天期美債殖利率
也可能維持高檔。不過，現階段企業獲利成長與 AI 相關需求仍具韌性，若第三季財報

延續前兩季優於預期的趨勢，企業 EPS 若能上修有望部分抵銷高利率對本益比的壓縮
效果。換言之，後續市場表現未必只由 Fed 政策主導，企業實際獲利能否持續兌現，
將是支撐科技與半導體股中期走勢的重要力量。
四、投資建議
1. 短線聚焦美股財報與 Fed 決策，科技股中期趨勢料不致有變：近期市場同時面臨財報
前的觀望態勢、美債殖利率偏高及 AI 資本支出疑慮等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能
受到壓抑，後續宜關注 10 月美股財報對企業獲利與資本支出展望，以及 FOMC 會議
所釋出的政策訊號。中期而言，目前 AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡

象，科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，但在利率維持高檔及市場評價偏高的環境下，後
續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出的實際變化。
2. 中天期債券風險報酬仍較均衡：美國 9 月 CPI 升溫使短端殖利率持續受到 Fed 政策路
徑影響，而 20～30 年超長端仍面臨財政赤字、國債供給與期限溢價偏高等結構性壓
力。相較之下，目前殖利率已位於歷史相對高檔，若後續核心通膨或能源價格改善，
中段殖利率具有回落條件，同時其存續期間風險又低於超長端，因此現階段以中天期
美國公債及投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

• Advanced packaging, full process and CPO. Within the US$8bn 2027 
target, wafer-to-substrate services should remain the largest contributor, 
while full-process packaging is expected to increase from roughly 
US$300m in 2026 to US$1bn in 2027. The more substantial full-process 
capacity increase is scheduled for 2H27. Packaging should represent 
approximately 75–80% of leading-edge revenue, implying US$1.6–2.0bn 
from testing. CPO could contribute approximately US$500m in 2027, 
based on publicly disclosed customer capacity plans.  For initial CPO 
projects, customers provide specialized equipment while ASE supplies 
factory space and operating personnel. This consignment model limits 
ASE’s capital exposure while standards are still evolving. Beyond NVIDIA, 
ASE is engaging other technology companies, hyperscalers and potential 
custom-silicon customers on CPO and other optical-packaging projects. 
Full-process investment is broader than any one product because chiplet 
architectures, HBM integration and rising foundry costs should increase 
demand for redistribution-layer-based multi-die packaging.
• GM outlook and pricing. ASE’s ATM gross margin has moved above its 
previous structural range. The former 30% ceiling is now viewed as the 
floor. Full-process packaging, leading-edge testing and other high-entry-
barrier services should be margin-accretive as they scale. The pricing 
environment also remains favorable. ASE is passing through higher

Mainstream utilization has also tightened outside smartphones, allowing ASE to recover incremental costs across

• Capex, capacity and principal risks. ASE expects approximately US$11.5bn of capex in 2026, comprising around
US$7bn for machinery and US$4.5bn for facilities and land. It is progressing 22 construction projects, with physical
space representing the most important bottleneck. Management expects 2027 capex to exceed 2026 because
investment is required across full-process packaging, wafer-to-substrate services, probing and testing. Debt

remains the preferred external funding source, supplemented by customer deposits and prepayments. Taiwan will
remain the center for high-end manufacturing, while some trailing-edge capacity is moving to Malaysia and
Singapore to provide geographic diversification and release constrained Taiwanese space.

ASE Technology Holding
Valuation
Our target price of NT$908 is based on 25x 2027 EPS average. Our PER is higher than its five-year range as we expect ASEH to
see accelerating earnings growth in the coming years with GM at upward expansion trend, mainly driven by its promising
outlook in LEAP business and strong expansion for long-term AI demand. At our target price, the shares would trade at a

2026/27E P/B of 10x/8x.
Risks
Key downside risks that could prevent ASE shares from reaching our target price include: 1) poor efficiency and less synergy
created from merger with SPIL; 2) failure to gain market share in advanced packaging; 3) industry oversupply due to aggressive
capacity expansion; 4) severe pricing erosion due to competition; and 5) a longer-than-expected semiconductor inventory
correction.
Prepared for Kevin Lu

Compensation is not linked to specific transactions or recommendations. Like all Firm employees, analysts receive
compensation that is impacted by overall Firm profitability which includes investment banking, sales and trading, and
principal trading revenues. One factor in equity research analyst compensation is arranging corporate access events between
institutional clients and the management teams of covered companies. Typically, company management is more likely to

participate when the analyst has a positive view of the company.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day

15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be
different from and does not affect a stock’s fundamental equity rating, which reflects a longer-term total absolute return

### Page 1

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

1 群益投顧 
精材(3374 TT)與 台 積 電 (2330 TT)簽 訂 先 進 封 裝 oS 產 能 外 包 協 議 ， 深 化 策 略 合 作 。 精
材 此 次 取 得 台 積 電 CoWoS 後段 oS 產 能 外 包 合 作 ， 對 長 期 業 務 結 構 升 級 具 正 面 意 義 ，
惟 股 價 於 公 告 前 已 連 續 6 個 交 易 日 上 漲 ， 累 計 漲 幅 約 40%，11/13/2026 收盤達 519 元
漲 停 ， 本 益 比 已 達 43 倍 ， 加 上 新 產 線 預 計 2029 年才開始第一期量產， 較 市 場 預 期
2027~2028 年量產晚， 短 期 獲 利 貢 獻 有 限 ， 且 仍 有 設 備 資 本 支 出 、 折 舊 及 CB 潛在稀
釋 風 險 ， 因 此 短 線 不 宜 追 價 。

研 究 員 ： 陳 俐 妍   
ly.c@capital.com.tw

台 積 電 擴 大 先 進 封 裝 外 包 布 局 ， 精 材 預 計 2029 年切入 CoWoS 後段 oS 製
程 ：  
精材(3374 TT) 於 11/13/2026 公 告 董 事 會 決 議 通 過 與 具 重 大 影 響 之 投 資 者 台
積電(2330 TT) 簽 訂 產 能 外 包 協 議 ， 契 約 期 間 自 2026/11/13 至 2033/12/31，
雙 方 將 針 對 先 進 封 裝 後 段 製 程 展 開 長 期 策 略 合 作。根 據 公 告 內 容，精 材 計 畫
進駐台積電中部科學園區廠房建置產線並負責生產營運，由台積電提供廠
房空間及先進封裝技術與製造能力支援，精材則自行投入生產設備及自動
物 料 搬 運 系 統 (AMHS)資 本 支 出，切 入 先 進 封 裝 供 應 鏈 中 的 oS(on-Substrate)
模 組 。 該 產 線 規 劃 於 2029 年 實 現 第 一 期 量 產 ， 雙 方 並 將 共 同 確 保 新 產 線 的
技 術 規 範、品 質 及 良 率 符 合 台 積 電 與 客 戶 要 求。此 外，精 材 董 事 會 同 步 通 過
發 行 新 台 幣 150 億 元 可 轉 換 公 司 債 (CB)， 以 支 應 初 期 產 能 建 置 需 求 。  
既 有 台 積 電 合 作 關 係 延 伸 至 先 進 封 裝 ， 有 助 精 材 拓 展 AI 及 HPC 業務：  
就 精 材 本 身 而 言，此 次 合 作 是 既 有 台 積 電 供 應 鏈 關 係 的 深 化。精 材 原 以 CIS
封 裝 及 晶 圓 級 封 裝 (WLCSP) 為 主 要 業 務，並 承 接 台 積 電 晶 圓 測 試 (CP)外 包 訂
單 ， 惟 既 有 測 試 機 台 於 08/2026 已 達 滿 載 ， 且 受 限 於 無 新 增 無 塵 室 空 間 ， 部
分 台 積 電 測 試 訂 單 轉 由 欣 銓 (3264 TT)承 接。此 次 透 過 進 駐 台 積 電 中 科 廠 區，
精材得以突破自身廠房空間限制，免除購地建廠所需的前置時間與土地成
本，並 利 用 台 積 電 既 有 廠 區 及 技 術 支 援，降 低 新 產 線 建 置 與 製 程 導 入 的 部 分
門 檻。由 於 台 積 電 目 前 持 有 精 材 約 41%股 權，雙 方 已 有 長 期 合 作 基 礎，此 次
進 一 步 由 晶 圓 測 試 及 既 有 封 裝 業 務 延 伸 至 CoWoS 後段 oS 製 程 ， 有 助 精 材
擴 大 與 台 積 電 的 合 作 範 圍，並 提 高 未 來 AI 及 HPC 相 關 業 務 的 營 收 占 比。不
過，CP 測 試 與 oS 屬 不 同 製 程，仍 須 觀 察 新 產 線 設 備 裝 機、製 程 驗 證 及 良 率
爬 升 進 度 。  
台 積 電 擴 大 先 進 封 裝 供 應 鏈 分 工 ， Interposer 與 oS 同 步 建 立 外 部 產 能 ：  
此 次 精 材 切 入 CoWoS 後段 oS 製 程 ， 應 放 在 台 積 電 持 續 擴 大 先 進 封 裝 供 應
鏈 分 工 的 整 體 策 略 下 觀 察 。 隨 AI 與 HPC 晶片朝 Chiplet、 HBM 及 異 質 整 合
發展，CoWoS 不 同 製 程 環 節 均 面 臨 產 能 擴 充 需 求 。 台 積 電 除 持 續 擴 充 自 身
先 進 封 裝 產 能 外，亦 逐 步 透 過 外 部 合 作 夥 伴 增 加 不 同 環 節 的 供 應 能 力，例 如
Interposer 方 面 與 GlobalFoundries 合 作 建 立 外 部 供 應 來 源，並 透 過 世 界 先 進
等 業 者 擴 充 相 關 產 能；後 段 先 進 封 裝 則 持 續 擴 大 與 OSAT 的 合 作，此 次 再 由
持股約 41%的 精 材 規 劃 於 台 積 電 中 科 廠 區 建 置 oS 產 線 ， 顯 示 台 積 電 正 逐 步
建 立 結 合 關 係 企 業 與 外 部 供 應 商 的 先 進 封 裝 供 應 鏈，以 提 高 產 能 配 置 彈 性。 
不 同 製 程 環 節 的 外 包 條 件 與 策 略 並 不 相 同 。 Interposer 外 包 主 要 在 於 增 加 供
應 來 源 及 產 能 彈 性；CoWoS 則 可 區 分 為 CoW 與 oS 兩 個 階 段，其 中 CoW 涉
及 晶 圓、矽 中 介 層 及 晶 片 間 的 高 精 度 整 合，製 程 複 雜 度 與 良 率 要 求 較 高，外

### Page 2

產業速報  
                                        2026 年 11 月 16 日

2 群益投顧 
包 仍 受 技 術 及 良 率 條 件 限 制；相 較 之 下，oS 主 要 負 責 將 完 成 的 CoW 模 組 接
合 至 基 板，隨 OSAT 業 者 製 程 經 驗 累 積 及 良 率 逐 步 成 熟，已 成 為 較 適 合 擴 大
委 外 的 環 節 ， 目 前 台 積 電 CoWoS 後段 oS 製 程 已 大 量 採 用 外 包 模 式 。 由 於
OSAT 的 先 進 封 裝 毛 利 率 通 常 低 於 台 積 電 整 體 製 造 業 務 ， 將 部 分 oS 產 能 交
由 外 部 業 者 承 接，亦 有 助 台 積 電 將 有 限 的 無 塵 室 空 間、設 備 及 工 程 資 源 優 先
配 置 於 技 術 門 檻 較 高 的 CoW 及 次 世 代 先 進 封 裝 製 程 。  
精材 2029 年 才 量 產 ， 2026～ 2028 年 既 有 OSAT 仍 持 續 受 惠 AI 封 裝 需 求 ：  
精 材 新 產 線 預 計 2029 年 才 開 始 第 一 期 量 產，因 此 在 2026～ 2028 年 間，並 不
會立即改變既有封測業者的供需格局。台積電雖可依製程整合需求向客戶
建 議 合 作 封 測 廠，但 客 戶 仍 可 依 自 身 需 求 直 接 與 封 測 業 者 洽 談 產 能 及 合 作，
因 此 先 進 封 裝 訂 單 並 非 完 全 由 台 積 電 單 方 面 分 配。在 AI/HPC 晶 片 持 續 推 動
先 進 封 裝 規 格 升 級 下，日 月 光 投 控、力 成 及 其 他 既 有 封 測 業 者 仍 可 持 續 受 惠
於新增外包需求，並透過製程改善、良率提升及設備效率優化增加有效產
出。 
至 2029 年 後 ， 精 材 oS 新 產 線 及 其 他 業 者 新 增 產 能 將 陸 續 開 出 ， 加 上 既 有
OSAT 持 續 透 過 良 率 改 善 增 加 有 效 產 能 ， 供 應 鏈 競 爭 可 能 逐 步 加 劇 。 因 此 ，
中 長 期 仍 須 觀 察 AI/HPC 需 求 成 長 能 否 持 續 吸 收 新 增 設 備 及 良 率 提 升 所 帶
來的有效供給，這將是影響先進封裝產業供需及各業者獲利表現的重要變
數。 
2029 年 後 新 增 有 效 產 能 可 能 增 加 ， 精 材 仍 面 臨 資 本 支 出 與 股 本 稀 釋 壓 力 ：  
雖然 AI/HPC 晶 片 持 續 朝 先 進 製 程、Chiplet 及 異 質 整 合 發 展，將 支 撐 先 進 封
裝 長 期 需 求 ， 但 目 前 CoWoS 供 不 應 求 ， 除 AI/HPC 需 求 快 速 成 長 、 晶 片 封
裝 面 積 擴 大 及 HBM 整 合 增 加 外 ， 製 程 複 雜 度 高 、 良 率 尚 待 提 升 ， 亦 可 能 限
制 有 效 產 能 釋 出。值 得 留 意 的 是，隨 製 程 技 術 持 續 改 進、設 備 調 校 及 量 產 經
驗 累 積，既 有 產 線 良 率 若 逐 步 提 升，即 使 未 新 增 設 備，也 可 能 增 加 有 效 產 出；
若 再 加 上 台 積 電 及 全 球 OSAT 業 者 持 續 擴 充 產 能 ， 未 來 供 給 增 幅 可 能 高 於
市 場 目 前 預 期 。 因 此 ， 現 階 段 CoWoS 供 不 應 求 未 必 能 長 期 延 續 ， 若 良 率 瓶
頸 逐 步 突 破，供 需 可 能 由 緊 俏 轉 向 平 衡，甚 至 出 現 供 給 較 為 充 裕 的 情 況，後
續 仍 須 觀 察 AI/HPC 需 求 成 長 能 否 吸 收 新 增 設 備 及 良 率 改 善 所 帶 來 的 有 效
供給。  
此 外 ， 精 材 須 自 行 負 擔 設 備 及 AMHS 資 本 支 出 ， 設 備 投 資 至 新 產 線 營 收 貢
獻 仍 有 時 間 落 差，設 備 陸 續 投 入 使 用 後 亦 將 增 加 折 舊 費 用，若 初 期 產 能 利 用
率 及 良 率 爬 升 不 如 預 期，可 能 對 毛 利 率 及 營 業 利 益 形 成 壓 力。資 金 方 面，精
材截至 11/2026 股 本 為 27.14 億 元 ， 約 當 2.714 億 股 ， 預 計 發 行 150 億元
CB， 目 前 尚 未 確 定 轉 換 價 格 ； 若 以 11/13/2026 收 盤 價 519 元 作 為 假 設 轉 換
價格，並以全數轉換估算，新增股數約 2,890 萬 股 ， 相 較 目 前 股 數 增 加 約
10.65%， 轉 換 後 原 股 東 持 股 比 例 將 稀 釋 約 9.63%。 惟 此 為 假 設 情 境 ， 實 際 稀
釋 程 度 仍 須 視 最 終 發 行 金 額、轉 換 價 格 及 轉 換 條 件 而 定。因 此，此 次 合 作 雖
有助精材長期業務結構升級，但仍須考量資本支出回收效益、折舊吸收能
力、CB 潛 在 稀 釋 及 未 來 產 業 供 需 變 化 ， 不 宜 直 接 以 目 前 CoWoS 供 不 應 求
的 狀 況 外 推 未 來 獲 利 。

3 群益投顧 
精 材 短 線 評 價 已 反 映 部 分 成 長 預 期 ， 封 測 股 則 可 留 意 修 正 後 的 布 局 機 會 ：  
精 材 此 次 取 得 台 積 電 CoWoS 後段 oS 產 能 外 包 合 作 ， 對 長 期 業 務 結 構 升 級
具 正 面 意 義 ， 惟 股 價 於 公 告 前 已 連 續 6 個 交 易 日 上 漲 ， 累 計 漲 幅 約 40%，
11/13/2026 收 盤 達 519 元 漲 停，本 益 比 已 達 43 倍，加 上 新 產 線 預 計 2029 年
才 開 始 第 一 期 量 產 ， 短 期 獲 利 貢 獻 有 限 ， 且 仍 有 設 備 資 本 支 出 、 折 舊 及 CB
潛 在 稀 釋 風 險 ， 因 此 短 線 不 宜 追 價 。  
另一方面，不宜因精材取得台積電合作，就立即下修日月光投控(3711 TT)
及力成(6239 TT) 的 中 短 期 投 資 看 法 。 2026 年至 2028 年 精 材 新 產 線 尚 未 量
產，既 有 封 測 廠 仍 可 持 續 承 接 AI 及 HPC 先 進 封 裝 與 測 試 外 溢 訂 單，並 透 過
產 線 效 率 及 良 率 改 善 提 升 有 效 產 出，中 短 期 應 以 訂 單 能 見 度、產 能 利 用 率 及
獲 利 表 現 作 為 主 要 觀 察 重 點。4Q26 以 來，部 分 封 測 股 已 因 市 場 對 AI 資 本 支
出 及 投 資 效 益 的 疑 慮 而 大 幅 修 正，若 後 續 訂 單 及 營 運 展 望 未 明 顯 轉 弱，評 價
修正反而可能提供布局機會，可持續留意受惠先進封裝需求的日月光投控
(3711 TT) 、力成(6239 TT)， 以 及 承 接 晶 圓 測 試 外 溢 需 求 的 欣 銓 (3264 TT)。
至 2029 年 後 ， 隨 精 材 及 其 他 業 者 新 增 產 能 陸 續 開 出 ， 加 上 既 有 產 線 良 率 提
升 帶 動 有 效 供 給 增 加 ， 則 須 留 意 供 需 變 化 及 競 爭 加 劇 風 險 。

4 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】  
  評等 定義 
  強力買進(Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 ≥ 35%  
  買進(Buy)  15%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <35%  
  區間操作(Trading Buy) 5%≤首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 <15%

中立(Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等  
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整  
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】  
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較 3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為 四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等 係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。   
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」 及 「 區 間 操 作 」 均有 upside 目 標 價 。 差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。  
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ：  
1. 達 目 標 價 。  
2. 雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。  
3. 推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】 
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

營收走勢圖

研究員/丁宥霖 yulin.ting@uni-psg.com

貿聯 3Q26 財 報 優 於 預 期 ， 營 業 利 益 率 創 歷 史 新 高 ， 加 上 
Interplex 併表效益優於原先預估，為後續營運增添新亮點。展
望未來，研究部持續看好貿聯中長期成長潛力，主要考量：(1) 隨 
AI 機櫃功耗持續提升，電力傳輸規格朝高電流、高壓化發展，帶
動 Power Whip、Busbar 等產品升級，單櫃電源產值可望倍增；
(2) 高速互連朝 1.6T 以上演進，AEC 與高階光纖業務接續放
量，推升資料傳輸產值；(3) Interplex 併購補足精密機構能力，
結合既有電力及高速互連布局，貿聯可望由零組件供應商逐步延
伸至機櫃次系統整合，擴大單一客戶供應範圍及長期成長商機。
研究部將 Interplex 納入預估，上修 2026、2027 年稀釋後 EPS 
至 73.49 元（+67.4% YoY，原估 68.77 元）及 145.24 元
（+103.2% YoY，原估 117.82 元）。惟考量近期 AI 資料中心建
置不確定性升高，可能使新平台導入與機櫃出貨時程遞延，壓抑
評價表現，研究部將本益比由 25 倍下修至 20 倍，以 2027 年 
EPS 為評價基礎，目標價 2,950 元，投資評等由「買進」調升至
「強力買進」。

重點摘要： 
1. 3Q26 營業利益率創歷史新高，財報優於預期 
貿聯 3Q26 營收 278.75 億元（+19.7% QoQ、+51.4% YoY），符
合預期。其中 HPC 營收成長強勁（+30% QoQ、+61% YoY），營收
占比由 2Q26 的 42% 提升至 46%。隨 AEC 拉貨恢復正常，上半
年產線未達經濟規模的不利影響逐步消除，帶動整體毛利率回升
至 32.53%（+1.80ppt QoQ、−0.72ppt YoY）；加上營收規模擴
大，營業費用率降至 11.75%（−1.76ppt QoQ、−1.97ppt YoY），
推升營業利益率至 20.78%（+3.56ppt QoQ、+1.24ppt YoY），創
歷史新高。營業利益達 57.92 億元（+44.5% QoQ、+61.0% YoY），
優於原估的 52.50 億元。業外因匯兌損失及利息費用合計損失 
2.30 億元，稅後淨利達 42.90 億元（+43.9% QoQ、+63.2% YoY），
優於原估的 39.78 億元，稀釋後 EPS 為 20.34 元，優於原估
的 19.45 元。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

2. Interplex 併表貢獻超預期，上修 4Q26 營收與獲利 
貿聯 10 月營收 118.46 億元（+26.9% MoM、+80.0% YoY），創
歷史新高，主要受惠 Interplex 於 9 月底完成交割，10 月首
度完整併表即貢獻營收 6,700 萬美元，約為併購公告時近 12 個
月平均月營收的一倍。Interplex 為 NVIDIA NVLink Switch 機
殼主要供應商，受惠 AI 機櫃需求強勁，併表貢獻高於原先預估。
考量併購效益優於預期，加上既有業務持續成長，研究部上修 
4Q26 營收至 349.76 億元（+25.5% QoQ、+76.8% YoY，原估 
302.69 億元）。預估毛利率為 32.49%（−0.04ppt QoQ、+0.56ppt 
YoY），大致持平前季；隨營收規模擴大及 Interplex 較低費用
率貢獻，營業費用率降至 10.85%，推升營業利益率至 21.64%
（+0.86ppt QoQ、+3.27ppt YoY，原估 20.0%）。預估 4Q26 稅
後淨利達 55.28 億元（+28.9% QoQ、+101.8% YoY），稀釋後 EPS 
由原估 22.38 元上修至 26.21 元（ +28.9% QoQ、+101.8% YoY）。

3. AI 機櫃供電架構升級，電源單櫃產值與市占率同步提升 
隨 AI 伺服器運算密度持續提高，機櫃功耗由 GB200 世代約 
120kW，朝 Vera Rubin 世代 200kW 以上發展，電力傳輸規格同
步升級。在既有低壓直流架構下，功耗提升帶動電流需求增加，
Power Whip 與 Busbar 須升級至更高載流規格，推升單櫃電源
產值；隨機櫃功率持續攀升，傳統 54VDC 架構面臨銅材用量、配
電損耗及機櫃空間限制，NVIDIA 已推動 800VDC 高壓直流架構，
透過提高配電電壓、減少電力轉換環節，支援未來 MW 級 AI 機
櫃需求。貿聯除受惠電源產品規格升級，於 Vera Rubin 世代的 
Busbar 市占率亦可望明顯提升，推動單櫃電源產值較 GB 世代
倍增。考量新平台相關出貨預計於 1H27 起逐季放量，研究部預
估 2027 年電源營收成長約 114% YoY；中長期隨 800VDC 架構
逐步導入，既有 Power Whip、Busbar 及高電流連接技術可望延
伸至新一代配電系統，持續擴大供應商機。

### Page 3

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  3

4. 高速互連升級至 1.6T，AEC 與高階光纖雙軌成長 
隨 AI 資料中心傳輸頻寬由 800G 朝 1.6T 以上升級，高速互連
對訊號完整性、傳輸距離及功耗的要求持續提高。短距離傳輸仍以
銅纜具成本優勢，較長距離則由光纖滿足頻寬與訊號傳輸需求，預
期 AEC 與光互連將持續並存。AEC 方面，1.6T 新規格因材料成本
及組裝難度提高，產品單價與毛利率可望優於前代，預計於 4Q26 
至 1Q27 逐步導入量產，研究部預估 2027 年 AEC 營收成長約 
41% YoY，主要受惠高階產品占比提升。光纖方面，新富生主要供
應 AI 資料中心機櫃間傳輸所需的 MPO、MT 高密度光纖跳線及連
接器組件，受惠高速光纖互連需求成長，月營收較 2026 年初成長
逾一倍，獲利同步改善。中長期來看，貿聯亦積極布局 Optical 
Shuffle、CPO 交換器方案及 Micro-VCSEL 光互連技術，逐步由傳
統線纜組裝延伸至高階光學組件，為後續成長開拓更多商機。

圖一、貿聯-KY 1Q25～4Q27F HPC 營收結構

資料來源：統一投顧預估

5. 半導體設備需求強勁，非 HPC 業務維持穩健 
貿聯 3Q26 半導體設備營收成長 11% QoQ、70% YoY，為非 HPC 業
務主要成長動能。隨先進製程及半導體設備投資持續推進，客戶需
求由傳統線束延伸至設備模組、精密組裝及次系統整合，帶動單機
供應產值提升。貿聯已深入薄膜沉積與蝕刻設備供應鏈，並逐步拓
展至微影及檢測設備，擴大產品應用範圍。其他業務方面，工廠自
動化、醫療、能源及車用等終端需求維持穩健，提供多元營收來源。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

6. 上修 2026～2028 年獲利預估，營運槓桿與併購效益持續擴
大 
研究部將 Interplex 併表貢獻納入預估，上修貿聯 2026 年營收
至 1,069.96 億元（+50.2% YoY，原估 1,021.18 億元），毛利率
估 31.4%，稀釋後 EPS 73.49 元（+67.4% YoY，原估 68.77 元）。
展望 2027 年，受惠 AI 電源及高速互連業務持續成長，加上 
Interplex 全年併表，預估營收達 1,710.84 億元（+59.9% YoY，
原估 1,517.46 億元）。隨高毛利 HPC 業務占比提升，毛利率可
望升至 34.3%（原估 34.0%）；加上營收規模擴大及 Interplex 
較低費用率貢獻，營業費用率降至 10.2%（原估 13.0%），推升營
業利益率至 24.1%（原估 21.0%），稀釋後 EPS 上修至 145.24 
元（+103.2% YoY，原估 117.82 元）。展望 2028 年，隨 AI 機
櫃供電架構朝 800VDC 演進、高速互連規格持續升級，加上 
Interplex 受惠機櫃需求成長及精密機構件供應範圍擴大，預期將
共同帶動營運成長。研究部預估營收達 2,136.69 億元（+24.9% 
YoY），稀釋後 EPS 196.20 元（+35.1% YoY，原估 171.03 元）。

圖二、貿聯-KY 2025～2028F 各應用別營收占比

資料來源：公司資料及統一投顧預估

7. 中長期成長趨勢明確，投資評等調升至「強力買進」 
研究部持續看好貿聯中長期成長潛力，隨 AI 機櫃電力與高速互
連規格持續升級，帶動單櫃供應產值提升，加上 Interplex 補足
精密機構能力，逐步擴大機櫃次系統供應範圍，有利深化客戶合作
關係並提升市占率。惟考量近期 AI 資料中心建置不確定性升高，
可能使新平台導入及機櫃出貨時程遞延，壓抑市場評價，研究部將
本益比由 25 倍下修至 20 倍。以 2027 年稀釋後 EPS 145.24 
元為評價基礎，目標價 2,950 元，投資評等由「買進」調升至「強
力買進」。

## Document 241: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26_ 조선사는 우직해야 한다HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26_ 조선사는 우직해야 한다3Q26_ 조선사는 우직해야 한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26_ 조선사는 우직해야 한다HD현대중공업(329180) 3Q26_ 조선사는 우직해야 한다3Q26_ 조선사는 우직해야 한다 - 기업.pdf

### Page 1

2026.10.29   
HD현대중공업  (329180)

3Q26: 조선사는 우직해야 한다  Company Brief

3분기 매출 5조 7,536억원/영업이익 8,992억원 
HD현대중공업의 2026년 3분기 매출은 5조 7,536억원/영업이익은 8,992억원으로 
컨센서스(6조 1,295억원/9,671억원) 및 당사 추정치(6조 859억원/9,679억원) 대비 
매출 및 영업이익 각각 -6.1%/-7.0%, -5.5%/-7.1%를 하회하는 실적을 기록하였다.  
조선사업부 매출이 4조 3,988억원/영업이익 6,463억원(OPM 14.7%)이며 이중 상선 
OPM은 14.7%로, 2분기 17.8%에 비해 3.1%P 하락하였으나 1분기 15.9%와 크게 
차이나지 않는 수준을 유지하면서 조업일수 및 환율 하락에도 불구하고 P-MIX및 
빈티지 개선에 따른 효과를 보여주었다. 해양사업부는 매출 3,799억원/영업이익 
1,265억원(OPM 33.3%)로 지난 분기(15.9%)보다도 훨씬 높은 영업이익을 
기록하였다. 엔진기계사업부는 매출 9,511억원/영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)를 
기록하며 역시 견조한 실적을 기록하였다.  
일회성 이익으로 Trion 공사의 C/O 180억원, 일회성 손실로는 셧다운에 따른 
조업감소 영향 500억원 및 환율하락에 따른 이익 감소분 450억원이 있었으며 금액은 
공개되지 않았으나 임단협 잠정타결에 따른 성과급/타결금 지급에 대한 추가 충당금의 
75% 인식이 반영되었다. 알려진 일회성만 제거하더라도 실질 영업이익률은 
17%이상으로 2분기 16.4%보다 높아진 호실적이라고 할 수 있다. 특히 RUYA 공사가 
본 궤도에 오르면서 해양사업부의 이익률이 괄목할 만큼 높아졌으며 지속가능하다는 
회사의 설명이다.

투자의견 BUY 및 조선업종 TOP PICK 유지, 목표주가 530,000원 하향 
HD현대중공업의 목표주가를 530,000원으로 38.3% 하향하지만, 투자의견 BUY와 
조선업종 TOP PICK을 유지한다. 환율 하락 및 28년 하반기 엔진증설 매출확대, 
해양공사 이익률 상승 등 요인을 종합 반영하여 실적 추정치를 미세 반영하여 도출한 
2027F BPS 131,910원에 Target P/B를 기존대비 하향한 4.0배로 적용하였다. 특수선 
기대감 및 선가상승 기대감 하락에 따른 조선업종 전반의 밸류에이션 하락을 반영하여 
Target을 낮췄지만 그럼에도 조선업종 중 가장 높은 멀티플을 부여하며 Top 
Pick의견을 견지한다. 여러 악재에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업은 탄탄하다. 본업인 
상선사업부의 9월말 기준 수주량은 137.9억 달러로 목표치를 이미 초과 달성하였으며 
전사 기준으로도 수주목표 90.5%를 채워넣었다. 수주잔고 또한 상선 421억 
달러/전사 609억 달러로 각각 작년 말 대비 12.6%/13.2% 증가하였다. 해양부문과 
엔진기계부문은 조선사업부보다도 높은 이익률로 든든한 실적의 기반이 되고 있다.  
최근 HD현대그룹은 미국의 EPC강자 Kiewit과 미국 합작법인 설립에 관한 계약을 
맺고 그린필드 기반의 미국 신규 조선소 건설을 추진하고 있다. 빠르면 올해 중으로 
건립과 관련한 세부 사항이 공개될 예정이며 HD현대중공업이 중추적인 역할을 담당할 
것으로 보인다. 일찍부터 브라운필드 방식으로 접근한 동종사와 달리 다소 시간은 
걸렸지만 과감하게 결정된 투자다. 조선소 예상 준공 역시 2028년 이후로 예상되어 
당장 기업가치에 반영하기는 어려우나 엔진 증설과 함께 2028년 후에는 분명한 실적 
상향 요인이 될 것으로 예상한다. 우직하게 본업에 충실하며 신중하게 미래를 준비하는 
조선소, 귀감이다.  
Buy (Maintain)

 당 보고서는 기관투자가 및 제 3자에게 E-mail등을 통하여 사전에 배포된 사실이 없습니다.  
 회사는 6개월간 해당 기업의 유가증권 발행과 관련 주관사로 참여하지 않았습니다. 
 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

## Document 251: [373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사...LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금_7b277d22

Source PDF: all report pdf/[373220] LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사...LG에너지솔루션(373220) 일회성 보상금_7b277d22.pdf

### Page 1

LG에너지솔루션 (373220)

Earnings Review Company Analysis | 이차전지/화학 | 2026. 11. 4  
일회성 보상금 포함된 실적, ‘30년 ESS 수요 대비 배 이상 높은 셀 3사 Capa 우려

AMPC 4,169억원 포함 영업이익 컨센서스 대폭 상회 
동사는 8일 매출액 9조 6,434억원(YoY 59%, QoQ 27%), AMPC 포함한 영업이익 
7,560억원(OPM 7.8%, YoY 26%, QoQ 567%)로 시장 Consensus(공정공시한 8일 
기준 3,264억원)을 대폭 상회하는 어닝 서프라이즈 발표. 회사는 ESS 출하 급증과 고
전압 Mid-Ni EV 파우치 확대, 견조한 EV향 2170-46시리즈 공급을 물량 중심을 성
장하였고, 폴란드 가동률 개선, 수익성이 견조한 원통형 물량 증가, 북미 ESS 생산성 
개선에 따른 고정비 부담 완화 및 고객사의 일회성 물량 보상금 영향으로 설명. 그러나 
일회성 물량 보상금의 구체적 금액, 반복 발생 여부는 제시하지 않아 불확실성 남겨. 
7,560억원의 영업이익에도 불구하고 영업외손실 9,872억원 발생으로 당기순손실 기
록. 이에는 소형 IT파우치 자산손상 반영되어, 가동률 개선 및 감가상각비 감소를 위해 
Capa를 조정하는 배터리 회사들의 대응 전략이 이번 실적에서 다시 확인

‘30년 ESS 수요 전망들 대비 국내사 Capa 배 이상 높아 
동사는 북미 ESS Capa 확대를 통한 탈중국 수요에 적극 대응하겠다는 전략. 파우치 
라인 추가 전환 및 신규 각형 라인 확보를 통해 북미 ESS Capa를 ‘26년말 50+에서 
‘27년 70+로 제시. 최근 타사에서 ‘26년 미국 ESS 수요를 80~100GWh, 혹은 
130+GWh로, ‘30년 두배 이상, 혹은 240+GWh로 수요 전망에 대해 답변한 바 있음. 
동사 전체 Capa 300GWh(‘25년 연말), SK On 179GWh, SDI 140GWh)임을 감안하
면 ‘30년 약 2배 수요를 가정하면 셀 3사 Capa의 32%에 달하는 수요, 만일 3배 수
요 가정하면 48.5% 수준. 북미 외 시장 가성비 중시 기조 및 중국 LFP 장악(‘25년 
97% M/S)으로 진입장벽 높음을 감안하면 미국 수요 중요. 이를 상쇄하기 위해 EV향 
배터리의 안정적인 공급 지속 필요하며, 이를 위해 동사 원통형은 TESLA의 비미국 시
장 판매량이, 파우치는 시장 자체 축소 지속 여부에 유의할 필요 
상대적으로 가장 견조한 출하량, 그러나 고평가 영역  
셀3사중 EV향 배터리 출하량 및 ESS 탑재에서 현재 가장 견조한 판매량을 보이고 있
으나, AMPC와 일회성 보상금이 영업손익의 상당부분을 견인한다는 점에서 수익성 취
약하다는 단점. 또한 ROE가 (-) 영역 임에도 PBR이 3.x내외로 거래되고 있어 고평가 
지적에서 자유롭기 어려워. 당분간 모회사인 LG화학의 PRS 발행에 따른 단기 오버행 
이슈도 관건. PBR 2.3x(‘25년대비 하락하는 ROE 감안, 20% 할인)적용 목표주가 
290,000원 제시, HOLD 유지. 주식 투자 매력 낮은 상황으로 판단 
 Analyst 정경희 
khchung@ls-sec.co.kr

LS Securities Research 2 
EV향 3분기 data  
이차전지에서 EV향 비중은 여전히 가장 높다. 타입별로 보면 3분기 글로벌 EV향 이차전
지에서 각형의 M/S가 83%로 지속 증가하고 있으며, 동사가 생산하는 원통형과 파우치의 
경우 합산 M/S가 17%로 하락하였다. 현재 Wh 기준 동사 EV향 배터리에서 3분기 파우
치가 55%, 원통형이 45%로 파우치 비중이 10% 원통형을 상회한다. 
그림1 LGES 원통형, 파우치 타입 EV 향 분기별 탑재량 추이 
그림2

자료: SNE Research(2026.10), LS증권 리서치센터

그림 2 EV 향 폼팩터 M/S: 각형 확장 vs 원통형/파우치 감소  그림 3 동사 EV 배터리 타입 비중

자료: SNE Research(2026.10), LS증권 리서치센터  자료: SNE Research(2026.10), LS증권 리서치센터

SNE data에 따르면 글로벌 원통형 EV향 수요의 대다수가 TESLA에서 발생하고 있으며, 
‘24~’25년 역성장후 ‘26년 판매량 회복을 나타내는 TESLA 호조로 전년대비 LGES의 EV
향 원통형 판매량은 3분기 증가했다. 반면 파우치의 경우 ‘25년 파우치 탑재 EV 판매 호조
를 나타낸 현대기아차그룹 영향으로 YoY 탑재량이 증가했으나, 올해는 북미 OEM인 GM, 
Ford향 판매가 IRA 일몰로 전년대비 부진함에 따라 3분기 전년대비 25% 감소한 것으로 
나타난다.

그림 9 셀 3 사 분기별 EV 향 탑재량 글로벌 M/S 추이: M/S 감소중

자료: SNE 리서치, LS증권 리서치센터

밸류에이션, 수익성 대비 고평가 영역  
동사는 ‘24년 ROE -4.9%, ‘25년 -5.2%에 이어 ‘26년 -4.4%, ‘27년 -3.4%로 (-) 영역이 
지속되는 것으로 예상한다. ‘24년과 ‘25년 순이익은 (+)지만, 해외 합작회사가 많고 이들 이
익을 연결함에 따라 지배주주순이익은 적자를 기록한 것을 보인다. JV 규모가 축소되고 있
어 이러한 순이익과 지배주주 순이익상의 괴리는 좁혀지겠지만, ROE가 (-)영역 지속을 예
상함에 따라 현재 거래 PBR은 고평가 상황으로 볼 수 있다. 물론 이것은 향후 수익 성장
에 대한 시장 기대가 반영된 것으로 볼 수 있지만, 이차전지 6개사 평균 Consensus 대비 
실제 수익이 ‘23년부터 지속 하향되고 있다는 점, ESS대비 여전히 판매비중이 높은 EV에서 
출하량 정체가 일어나고 있다는 점, 미국 ESS에서 여전히 높은 중국 M/S를 감안하면 이러
한 기대 수준에 대해 다시 검토할 필요가 있다. 이에 더해, 모회사 기준에서는 필요한 과정
이지만 동사 주가 측면에서는 LG화학의 PRS 발행도 단기 오버행으로 당분간 주가에 부담
을 줄 수 있다.

그림 10 셀 3 사, 양∙음극재 3 사 추정치 변화율: ‘23 년부터 추정치 지속 하향 조정

자료: DataGuide, LS증권 리서치센터

미국 ESS가 모든 것을 해결할 수 있나? 
‘25년 글로벌 ESS 시장의 97%를 중국배터리사가 공급했다. LFP 각형이 표준화된 시장인 
것이다. 미국 시장만 중국에 대한 관세 허들, 정책 허들로 한국 배터리사에 기회로 작용한
다. 그러나 ‘26년부터 시작된 FEOC와 관세추가 부담에도 불구, 상반기 미국 ESS 시장의 
76%을 (기타사의 국적을 알 수 없기에 제외하더라도) 중국 셀사가 차지했다. 만일 한국 배
터리 유관회사들이 전망하듯 ‘30년 미국 ESS 시장이 160~200GWh 이상, 240+GWh, 혹
은 240~300GWh가 된다고 해도 3사 전체 Capa의 50%가 채 되지 못한다. EV 수요가 뒷
받침되지 않는다면 미국 ESS만으로는 가동률을 80~90% 이상 올리기 어렵다는 의미다. 특
히 동사는 원통형과 파우치를 생산하고 있으나, 원통형은 북미외 지역 TESLA의 판매에, 
파우치는 올해 출하량이 감소하는 것으로 나타나고 있다. 파우치 고객의 각형 채택 비중 
증가를 감안하면 동사 EV향 배터리 출하량 증가를 확신하기는 어렵다.   
긍정적 기대와 동시에 냉정한 점검이 필요한 시점으로 판단한다.

LS Securities Research 6 
표2 LG 에너지솔루션 3Q26 실적발표 주요 Q&A 
[Q&A]           
Q1-1. 4Q26 실적 전망과 2027 년 큰 방향은? 
  A. 4Q26 ESS 출하량은 북미 수요 대응으로 QoQ 약 40% 증가 예상 
   유럽 고전압 Mid-Ni 파우치 및 원통형 물량도 연말까지 견조한 출하 지속 전망 
   3Q26 가동을 시작한 북미 JV 효과가 더해져 전사 매출은 QoQ 약 10% 성장 가능할 것으로 예상 
   가동률 개선과 사이트 안정적 램프업은 지속 추진하나 3Q26 일회성 요인 소멸의 기저효과 존재 
   4Q26 혼다 JV ESS CAPA 추가 가동 준비에 따른 초기 고정비 부담으로 수익성 일부 둔화 불가피

2027 년 사업계획은 수립 중이며 전방 수요 변수로 구체적 가이던스 제시는 어려운 단계 
    ESS: 북미 확보 수주 기반으로 유의미한 물량 성장 지속 기대 
    소형: 전략 고객향 EV 원통형과 46 시리즈 신규 애플리케이션 판매로 안정적 성장 예상 
    EV 파우치: 중저가 제품 물량과 북미 EV 향 생산품이 더해져 성장 예상 
   전 사업부 매출 성장 기대하나 핵심 제품 경쟁력·기존 CAPA 효율화·가동률 극대화·원가 개선에 집중 
   2027 년 매출 성장률·영업이익률 등 구체적 수치는 제시 X

Q1-2. 북미 PFE 요건 충족을 위한 LFP 공급망 전략과 중국 관련 주력 양극재 공급사 대응은? 
  A. 미국 생산세액공제 확보를 위해 직접재료비 내 PFE 소재 비중 요건을 충족하는 공급망 구축 필요 
   회사 설명상 PFE 소재 허용 비중은 2026 년 40%에서 2027 년 35% 이하로 강화. Non-PFE 비중과 혼동 유의 
   소재별 소싱 전략을 구체화해 준비 중이며 요건 충족은 차질 없이 진행될 것으로 판단 
   재료비 비중이 큰 양극재는 최근 계약한 국내 업체 외 복수 Non-PFE 업체 조달 옵션 확보

음극재·분리막·전해액도 기존 Non-PFE 공급망에 더해 신규 업체 적극 발굴 
   기존 원가 중심 최적화에서 정책·지정학 리스크를 고려한 공급망 안정성 강화로 전략 확대 
   협력사의 원가·기술·물량 대응력을 종합 평가하고 장기적으로 현지 협력사 발굴·현지화 추진 
   질문한 중국 관련 주력 공급사의 개별 지위·조달 비중에 대한 직접적인 상세 답변 X

Q2-1. ESS 사업의 수익성 전망 및 개선 방안은? 
  A. EV→ESS 라인 전환으로 수요에 맞는 생산체계를 구축하고 북미 고객 적기 공급에 주력 
   다수 사이트 동시 램프업에 어려움이 있었고 고정비 절감 노력에도 수익성 개선 속도는 시장 기대보다 느렸음 
   2026 년 말 50GWh 목표 셀 라인 램프업은 마무리 국면. Link CAPA 도 3Q26 에 이어 4Q26 추가 보강 예정 
   CAPA 안정화와 점진적인 고정비 부담 완화 기대 
   확보 CAPA 효율화, 소재 소싱 다변화로 원재료비·물류비 절감에 집중 
   AI 데이터센터 및 SI 프로젝트 비중 확대를 통한 고객 포트폴리오 최적화 추진 
   셀·시스템 고용량화로 원가 경쟁력을 높여 구조적 수익성 개선 추진 
   구체적 ESS 영업이익률 목표나 개선 폭은 제시 X

Q2-2. 북미 ESS 추가 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 빠른 시장 성장 및 Non-PFE 에 우호적인 정책으로 북미 현지 생산 셀 수요 급증 
   현재 계획된 CAPA 기준으로 2028 년까지 물량은 대부분 확정. 그 이후 물량 중심으로 고객 수주 협의 진행 
   기존 전력망·유틸리티 고객, 이들을 통한 AI 데이터센터 BESS 수요, 하이퍼스케일러 BTM 직접 구매 수요 포함 
   예상 수요가 공급 가능 CAPA 를 초과해 전략적으로 중요한 프로젝트 중심으로 미래 잔여 CAPA 운영 계획 
   추가 고객명·신규 계약량 등은 제시하지 않음. 예상 수요와 확정 수주를 구분

Q3-1. 46 시리즈의 구체적인 수주 현황과 향후 모멘텀은? 
  A. 기존 OEM 부터 EV 스타트업까지 이미 440GWh 이상 물량 확보 
   추가로 다수 글로벌 OEM 과 협의 중이며 가까운 시일 내 의미 있는 규모의 수주 성과 기대 
   제품 경쟁력·지역별 생산 역량으로 46 시리즈 선도 입지를 강화하고 고객 포트폴리오 확대 계획 
   440GWh 는 확보 물량이며 연간 판매량이나 생산 CAPA 가 아님. 추가 계약 규모·고객명은 미공개

### Page 7

Company Report / LG에너지솔루션 / 2026. 11.4 
LS Securities Research 7

Q3-2. Tabless 2170 의 경쟁력과 수주·신규 시장 대응 현황은? 
  A. 기존 제품 대비 낮은 발열·저항으로 안전성 및 고출력에 유리해 HEV·BBU·로봇 등 다양한 용도에 적합 
   오창에서 전동공구용 제품은 이미 양산 중  
   BBU 향 LFP 2170 은 2Q27 양산 목표로 준비 중 
   HEV 향은 2027 년 말 애리조나에서 양산 계획  
   LFP 2170 은 NCM 대비 열안전성 테스트에서 우수한 성능을 보여 고객의 긍정적 피드백 확보 
   고객 니즈·수요에 적극 대응해 신시장 경쟁력 확보 추진. 수주 규모 등 정량 정보는 제시하지 않음

Q4-1. 재무 건전성 개선 방안과 2027 년 자금 조달 계획은? 
  A. 선제 CAPA 투자 자금은 저리 정책자금 장기차입 및 원화·외화 회사채로 안정적으로 조달해 왔음 
   주요 투자는 대부분 집행됐고 CAPEX 는 2025 년을 기점으로 유의미하게 감소 중 
   향후 영업현금흐름 확대분을 차입금 상환에 우선 활용, 자산 유동화를 통한 추가 재원 확보도 병행 
   차입금·금융비용 부담을 축소해 재무지표 개선 및 건전성 확보 추진 
   2026 년 본사 추가 차입 계획은 없으며 해외법인은 기존 약정 차입금 일부 추가 인출 예정 
   2027 년은 기존 차입금 차환·운전자금 범위에서 최소한의 자금 조달 계획 
   영업현금 확대·투자 부담 완화에 따른 잉여 재원으로 차입금 감축 기조 지속. 구체적 조달 규모는 미제시

Q4-2. 유럽 IAA 정책 방향에 대응한 중장기 점유율 확보 전략은? 
  A. IAA 는 아직 최종안 미확정이나 수정 권고안상 외국인 직접투자·현지화 요건 강화 예상 
   배터리뿐 아니라 주요 소재의 현지 생산 역량 중요성이 확대될 전망 
   유럽 OEM 의 공급망 다변화로 현지 생산 체계를 갖춘 비중국 업체 소싱 니즈 확대, 경쟁 환경은 우호적 판단 
   폴란드 현지 양산 및 소재·부품 공급망을 이미 구축해 현지 배터리 소싱 수요에 신속 대응 가능 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 에 대한 유럽 고객 관심 증가. 폴란드 감가상각비 감소도 가격 경쟁력 확보에 기여 
   고전압 Mid-Ni 외 LFP·CTP 등 다양한 제품·솔루션으로 신규 수주를 확보해 유럽 점유율 확대 추진

자료: LS증권 리서치센터

## Document 269: An RBA peak of greater conviction (2026-11-27)

Source PDF: all report pdf/An RBA peak of greater conviction (2026-11-27).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #rba
An RBA peak of greater conviction
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
08:56 November 13 2026 Share
We already believed the RBA cash rate was likely at a peak, even if RBA officials were less
convinced. A deeper housing downturn and early signs of cracks in the consumer sector
point to an even softer 2027. Fading inflation risks from oil also boost our conviction on
rates being at a peak.

### Page 2

It was already our view that the RBA was likely done hiking the cash rate after the November
increase. Since then, our conviction on that call has strengthened. Later today we will publish
the November Market Outlook with revised forecasts. GDP growth, the labour market and housing
market have all been revised down in 2027. Underlying inflation has been revised down slightly in the
second half of 2027, bringing the year-ended figure for calendar 2027 more decisively inside the

RBA’s 2–3% target range than our previous forecast round. The unemployment rate forecast has
been revised up to peak above 5%. Based on this revised forecast profile, an increase in the cash
rate from here would be unlikely and – we would argue – undesirable.
A range of considerations underlie this forecast revision. The housing market has undershot our
forecasts over the past couple of months. Turnover and financing activity have been coming in
under our earlier expectations. Mortgage loan inquiries have taken another leg down in October after

the September rate hike, after finding something of a level in prior months, with the additional impact
of the November rate hike still to come.
We are also seeing early signs of a turn in the consumer. Consumer income and spending have
so far been quite resilient to pressures from fuel prices and rising interest rates. But our Westpac
Card Tracker is starting to show that discretionary services spending is turning down again in Q4,
joining the discretionary goods category that was already quite weak. We would not over-emphasise

this point, but it does imply that the risks of further surprising resilience are fading.
Subsequent to the November Monetary Policy Board (MPB) meeting, business survey data
has shown weaker conditions and a sharp decline in forward orders. They also show further
evidence that current inflation is mostly a non-labour cost shock that is not being fully
passed through to consumer prices.
As we noted following the November MPB meeting, oil and fuel prices are already materially

lower than the track assumed in the RBA’s forecasts. Dated Brent is nearly one-fourth lower than
on 28 October, the date the RBA’s forecasts were finalised, and spot Brent is around 15% lower. This
significantly lowers the risk of a new wave of pass-through of fuel costs to other prices, as well as
easing consumer inflation perceptions and expectations.
In the media conference following the November meeting, Governor Bullock flagged that it was a

good time to “take stock” of the policy tightening to date. Both we and the market read this as a
signal that the MPB would pause at the December meeting. The 6:3 split vote at the meeting also
suggests that some external members are more attentive to downside risks than the internal
members.
A rate hike in February 2027 cannot be ruled out but it is looking even less likely given signs
in the data and surveys. The MPB will at least discuss a hike in February. The staff and senior

officials remain sensitive to inflation risks and have a downbeat view of supply capacity growth.
Given what we are seeing in the economy now, though, a rate hike in 2027 would require
either a material new inflation shock that has not yet occurred, or would constitute a policy
error on the part of the RBA (something that we will never forecast as a base case, knowing the
talents and capabilities of the staff and the Board). We concur with the dissenting voters in the MPB

that the downside risks need more attention. Policy is clearly restrictive, and the risk of overdoing the
inflation correction is growing. And by the time we get to February, we think there will be more
evidence that the domestic economy is slowing materially, and the inflation outlook with it.

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -16.26 điểm tương ứng -0.93%, với thanh khoản mở 
rộng. Thiết bị dụng cụ Y tế, Tiện ích khí và Bảo hiểm là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt 
nhất trong tuần qua. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị 
-870 tỷ đồng, tập trung ở các mã MBB, VIC và VCB; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng 
ở mức 504 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng -2,010 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua 
và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -10,888 hợp đồng. Tự doanh duy trì mua ròng 1,208 hợp 
đồng nhưng đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -4,070 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam có tuần giảm điểm thứ 2 liên tiếp khi VN-Index lùi về kiểm định vùng hỗ trợ 
quanh 1.725 điểm, với phiên cuối tuần có thời điểm giảm hơn 30 điểm trước khi thu hẹp 
đà giảm nhờ lực cầu ở nhóm chứng khoán. Áp lực bán tập trung ở nhóm vốn hóa lớn, đặc 
biệt là bộ đôi Vingroup (VIC, VHM) cùng một số cổ phiếu ngân hàng và công nghệ, khiến 
chỉ số thiếu lực đỡ. Giao dịch của khối ngoại diễn biến trái chiều và giằng co — có phiên 
mua ròng trở lại ở một số mã, nhưng vẫn xen kẽ bán ròng ở nhóm trụ như VHM, VCB, cho 
thấy dòng vốn ngoại chưa xác lập xu hướng rõ ràng. Bối cảnh bên ngoài kém thuận lợi khi 
nhóm cổ phiếu công nghệ tại Mỹ bị bán tháo, kéo theo các chỉ số chính toàn cầu đồng 
loạt lao dốc. Mặc dù vậy, kỳ vọng vào động thái mở kênh hoán đổi ngoại tệ của NHNN để 
hỗ trợ thanh khoản, hạ lãi suất đã giúp tâm lý nhà đầu tư trong nước ổn định và không 
xuất hiện áp lực bán tháo.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tuần qua hình thành mẫu nến spinning thân nhỏ với bóng trên/dưới khá dài, 
cùng thanh khoản gia tăng, thể hiện sự giằng co mạnh nhưng về tổng thể lại cho thấy diễn 
biến đã cân bằng trở lại sau tuần giảm điểm trước đó. Trên khung ngày, mặc dù trải qua 
các phiên biến động tăng/giảm mạnh đan xen nhưng việc cho phản ứng bật tăng tích cực 
tại quanh đường biên dưới của mẫu hình tam giác đi ngang trung hạn cho thấy tương quan 
vẫn đang nghiêng về chiều hướng tăng. Diễn biến phân hóa và biến động mạnh có thể còn 
tiếp diễn, tuy nhiên khi xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn vẫn đang được bảo lưu, chỉ số được kỳ 
vọng sẽ tiếp tục hướng lên thử thách lại các vùng cản phía trên.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên)

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

- Sau khi phủ sóng các thành phố lớn, chuỗi Long Châu đang mở rộng sang các vùng cấp 2, 3.

## Document 295: CEE & CCA week ahead_ Rate decisions in Poland, Kazakhstan to accompany GDP and inflation data

Source PDF: all report pdf/CEE & CCA week ahead_ Rate decisions in Poland, Kazakhstan to accompany GDP and inflation data.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The National Bank of Poland is expected to keep rates
unchanged, while Kazakhstan faces a close rate decision. GDP
and inﬂation data releases will also make for a busy week
across the region

Adam Antoniak, Peter Virovacz, Muhammet Mercan and Dmitry
Dolgin
We're expecting the National Bank of Poland to keep rates on hold on 2
December
Poland: NBP expected to keep rates
unchanged
On Monday, ﬂash November CPI is likely to show that consumer
inﬂation moderated slightly in Poland, as oil prices and,
consequently, retail petrol prices have been falling in recent weeks.
We estimate that food prices increased after ﬁve consecutive
months of year-on-year declines, while core inﬂation rose back
above 3%, mainly due to a low reference base from November
2025, when core prices declined by 0.1% month-on-month.

### Page 2

Also on Monday, the 3Q26 GDP composition should show
somewhat slower consumption growth amid moderating real wage
growth and pressure from high petrol prices. Meanwhile, ﬁxed
investment likely remained robust, as projects ﬁnanced under the
Domestic Recovery and Resilience Programme were in full swing.
The contribution from net exports was probably weaker than the
0.5ppt reported in 2Q26.
On Wednesday, the National Bank of Poland should keep interest

rates on hold, with the main policy rate remaining at 3.75%. Polish
monetary authorities usually refrain from changing interest rates in
December unless there is an urgent need for a policy adjustment.
Core inﬂation was 0.0% MoM in each of the last two months,
suggesting no indirect impact from higher fuel prices on broader
inﬂation. The government is preparing measures to freeze
household gas and electricity bills in 2027, while oil prices have
moderated. CPI inﬂation is therefore likely to be lower than

presented in the central bank’s November projection.
Hungary: Third-quarter growth likely to
have been sluggish
Hungary's detailed GDP breakdown is due on Tuesday and should
conﬁrm sluggish third-quarter growth. Net exports could surprise
on the positive side, while investment activity was probably the
main drag and consumption the key growth engine. On the
production side, a sharp fall in agriculture and weakness in
construction should be oﬀset by industry and services. A minor

upward revision to the ﬂash estimate is also on the cards.
Thursday brings the November budget data. So far, so good for this
year’s budget execution. Another manageable monthly deﬁcit in
November should be followed by a signiﬁcant improvement in
December as EU funds ﬂow in. The revised full-year target therefore
looks achievable, with the Maastricht deﬁcit potentially even
outperforming the 7.5% of GDP plan.
Turkey: Third-quarter growth expected at

2.9%
Your privacy is important

### Page 3

Third-quarter GDP data is due on Monday. We expect year-on-year
growth of 2.9%, on the back of resilient services and a relative
recovery in the construction sector despite sluggish performance in
the industry. While early indicators implied a further softening in
4Q26 domestic demand, we currently see full-year GDP growth at
2.8%, with risks tilted to the downside.
November CPI follows on Thursday, when we expect inﬂation to
come in at 0.8% MoM and 29.7% YoY, versus 29.8% a month ago,

supported by the recent decline in energy prices. Risks are on the
upside given pressure on food prices.
CCA: Armenia inﬂation and Kazakhstan's
rate decision
Armenia's November CPI is due on Thursday. We expect November
inﬂation to accelerate noticeably from 4.3% to 4.7% YoY, partly due
to adverse base eﬀects, and partly reﬂecting a genuine increase in
price pressures. Such a print would strengthen our expectation that
the Central Bank of Armenia will deliver another rate hike in

December, following its recent shift towards tighter policy. The
composition will also be important: food inﬂation moderated in
October, while housing and services pressures strengthened despite
the continued support from a strong dram and a somewhat more
restrained ﬁscal policy. A further broadening of domestic price
pressures would provide a strong argument for another rate
increase.
Kazakhstan's base rate decision comes on Friday. We expect the

National Bank of Kazakhstan to cut the base rate by 25bp to
15.50%, although we see this as a relatively low-conviction call,
with a hold the main alternative. Further easing would be
supported by the broader disinﬂation trend, some moderation in
inﬂation expectations and still-high real rates. At the same time,
sticky services inﬂation, a less supportive tenge and persistent
ﬁscal and tariﬀ-related risks argue for caution, while the NBK
previously signalled a low probability of another rate move before

year-end.

## Document 315: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ November 23-27

Source PDF: all report pdf/ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD_ November 23-27.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-23
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: November 23-27
Date:    1 hour ago
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD: November 23-27

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
ECONOMIC WEEK AHEAD:
November 23-27
By Ed Yardeni & Toby Hearst • 22 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Monica Sauro / Unsplash

### Page 3

Last week, the S&P 500 fell 8.6% to below 7,000, its 12th worst week
since 1952, and the Nasdaq 100 lost 10.4%. The selloff came despite
strong earnings from Nvidia and Anthropic's successful IPO.
Investors will be glad to see a holiday-shortened week, with markets
closed Thursday for Thanksgiving and closing early Friday. The calendar
of economic releases is light but includes the flash PMIs, PCED, GDP, and
jobless claims. Dell, HP, and Analog Devices report earnings (Tue). Here's

more:
(1) PCED. October’s core PCED (Wed) follows September’s 0.3% m/m
gain. The Cleveland Fed’s Inflation Nowcasting tracking model projects
another 0.3% m/m for both core and headline PCED. That would lift the
y/y rates to 3.2% and 3.8% from September’s 3.1% and 3.6%. The core
PPI final demand for personal consumption rose 4.8% y/y in October, well
above the core CPI’s 2.6% (chart).
PCED computers and peripheral equipment rose 9.7% y/y in September,

up from August’s 8.4%. PPI electronic components rose faster, to 31.8%

### Page 4

in October from September’s 26.8%, widening the gap (chart). That points
to a continuation of elevated inflation in tech goods in the coming months.
(2) GDP. The second estimate of Q3 GDP (Wed) follows the advance
estimate of 3.4% saar, with the GDP price index up 2.3%. The Atlanta
Fed’s GDPNow model estimates Q4 growth at 3.2% as of November 18,
led by a 12.2% jump in business equipment spending (chart).
A modest cyclical slowdown is possible through mid-2027. Growth has

rested on a robust consumer, strong business spending, and a federal
deficit near 6% of GDP during an expansion. Both consumer spending
and business investment are likely to slow, with data center activity at risk
after the midterms. Earnings growth should slow in tandem. The slowdown
should be modest. It means the Fed won’t need to move far into restrictive

### Page 5

territory, but it still needs to get back to neutral with a 50bps increase in
the federal funds rate, in our opinion.
(3) ADP & unemployment claims. ADP’s weekly employment reading
(Tue) follows a four-week average of 23,750 for the week ended October
31, up from 19,250 (chart).
Initial jobless claims (Wed) follow last week’s 203,000, up from 202,000,
with the four-week average at 197,750 (chart). Continuing claims rose to
1,763,000 for the week ended November 6, above the four-week average

of 1,709,000.
(4) Flash PMIs. S&P Global’s flash M-PMI and NM-PMI for November
(Mon) should show some easing. The available US regional business

### Page 6

surveys suggest that manufacturing weakened slightly in November
(chart).
But they also suggest that manufacturing employment strengthened in
November (chart).
Eurozone and UK flash PMIs also land Monday. October’s final M-PMIs
were 52.7 for the Eurozone and 52.8 for the UK (chart).

### Page 7

(5) South Korea. The Bank of Korea's rate decision (Thu) follows a base
rate of 3.00% in October, with markets pricing a 77% chance of a hike
(chart). October's CPI inflation was 2.8% y/y, and the month’s PPI inflation
was 7.2%.
South Korea is a key pillar of the AI trade. Its exports rose 93.9% y/y in
October. The KOSPI fell to 4,870 on Friday, well below its 200-day moving
average of 6,626.7 (chart). The KOSPI is now down more than 45% from
its June peak.

### Page 8

clause.

WESTPAC ECONOMICS  
OCTOBER CPI PREVIEW
Inflationary pressure fades as we move through year-end
17 November 2026
Key points
• October CPI to rise 0.41% with the annual 
pace lifting to 4.8%yr. The seasonally 
adjusted CPI lifts 0.7%mth, a small step 
up from the 0.6% increase in September.  
• October Trimmed Mean (TM) is set 
to gain 0.42%mth lifting the annual 
pace to 3.7%yr from 3.6%yr. 
• There is a small downside revision 
from our October 19th estimate for 
the December quarter CPI to 1.2%qtr 
while the TM remains 0.9%qtr.  
• The December quarter will represent the 
peak in Australian inflationary pressure. 
Our March quarter CPI remains 0.4%qtr 
and the TM is unchanged at 0.7%qtr. 
• We now see TM inflation dipping below 
3% by Q3 2027 and be well within the 
RBA’s target band by end 2027 at 2.7%yr.
• Demand remains the larger driver of 
inflation, but the recent pick-up in 
trimmed mean inflation appears to 
have been largely supply-driven.

### Page 2

Past performance is not a reliable indicator of future performance. The forecasts given above are predictive in character. Whilst every effort has been taken to ensure that the assumptions on which the forecasts are based are reasonable, 
the forecasts may be affected by incorrect assumptions or by known or unknown risks and uncertainties. The results ultimately achieved may differ substantially from these forecasts.
2
WESTPAC ECONOMICS
BULLETIN
Inflation accelerated in September...
The CPI rose 0.8% in the month of September, stronger than 
Westpac’s 0.6% estimate and the market’s expectation of 
0.7%. The annual pace picked up from 4.0%yr to 4.3%yr. The 
seasonally adjusted CPI rose 0.6% in September highlighting 
some positive seasonality in the month. 
Key factors driving the headline monthly result were: auto 
fuels (9.4%mth); domestic holiday travel & accommodation 
(8.0%mth); international holiday travel & accommodation 
(7.2%mth); and property rates & charges (5.5%mth). There 
were modest offsets from: women’s garments (–3.1%mth); 
household textiles (–6.6%mth); and accessories (–4.1%mth).
The more important underlying measure, the Trimmed Mean 
(TM), gained 0.3% in September, spot on both Westpac and 
market expectations with the annual pace lifting from 3.5%yr 
(revised from 3.6%yr) to 3.6%yr. The six-month annualised 
pace is now 4.0%yr the fastest in the short history of this 
series.
Overall, the breadth of inflation moderated for a second 
consecutive month. The share of the basket running above 
3%yr fell from 40% to 35%, while the share below 2%yr 
rose from 27% to 32%. Meanwhile, our replication of the San 
Francisco Fed’s inflation shock momentum index moved into 
positive territory after trending higher since April. Around 
14% of the CPI basket, on an expenditure-weighted basis, 
experienced positive inflation shocks in September, driven 
by holiday travel, fuel, and clothing & footwear. This was 
partly offset by negative shocks across 7% of the basket, 
concentrated in food & non-alcoholic beverages. For full 
analysis of the September CPI, see “Inflation accelerating in 
the run up to Christmas”.
... but is set to peak in October.
Looking ahead to October, early indicators were mixed. The 
NAB business survey suggested firms continued to absorb 
a meaningful share of higher input costs, with purchase cost 
growth edging up to 2.4%qtr (+0.1ppt from September), while 
retail price growth slowed sharply to 0.2%qtr (–0.8ppts), 
partly reflecting some moderation in labour cost growth 
(see here). Meanwhile, consumers’ year-ahead inflation 
expectations rose to 5.3% in October, with this rate holding 
steady in November. Fuel prices provided a more pronounced 
source of inflationary pressure, with retail petrol and diesel 
prices jumping 8.7% and 5.3%, respectively, in October to 
average $2.40/l and $2.82/l as global oil prices approached a 
local peak.
October to mark the peak in inflation

BULLETIN
We expect the October CPI to rise 0.4% (0.41% to 2dp), lifting
annual inflation to 4.8%, which we expect to mark the peak.
On a seasonally adjusted basis, we estimate a stronger 0.7%
increase (0.68% to 2dp). For the monthly trimmed mean, we

expect a 0.4% rise (0.42% to 2dp), nudging the annual pace up
to 3.7%. On a 3m/3m basis, the TM is tracking a 1.0% increase
in October. While conceptually different from the quarterly
trimmed mean, this measure nonetheless provides a useful
guide to near-term underlying inflation momentum. For market
services excluding volatiles & holiday travel, we have pencilled
in a 0.3% rise, lifting annual inflation to 4.0% from 3.8%,
which had marked a ten-month low.

Transport is expected to make the largest contribution
(+0.35ppts), almost entirely reflecting auto fuel (+0.32ppts).
Smaller contributions are expected from clothing & footwear
(+0.06ppts), household goods & services (+0.05ppts), and
meals out & takeaway (+0.04ppts). The main offset comes
from recreation & culture (–0.18ppts), driven entirely by
holiday travel (–0.18ppts), while fruit & vegetables, utilities
and several other components provide only marginal offsets.

Key details for the month of October
Food & non-alcoholic beverages are expected to rise 0.2% in
October, lifting annual inflation to 3.3%. Strength in the month
is concentrated in meals out & takeaway, where we expect
prices to rise 0.6%mth and 4.3%yr. We have allowed for a
greater-than-usual degree of price resetting as businesses
adjust to the card surcharging ban.
Housing inflation is expected to rise 0.1% in October, with the
annual pace increasing to 6.2% reflecting base effects from

electricity. Across the major components:
• Rents are expected to rise 0.2%mth and 3.5%yr, with the
late-September indexation of the Commonwealth Rent
Assistance flowing through more broadly in October. Our
seasonally adjusted estimate is stronger at 0.4%mth.
• New dwelling inflation has been a key source of downside
surprise in recent months. We expect a 0.2%mth rise
in October, which would see the annual pace moderate
to 4.5%. The downturn in established home prices has

deepened into year-end (see here), which should weigh on
new dwelling inflation over the months ahead. Our current
estimates point to a trough of around 1.3%yr by late-2027.
• Maintenance & repair costs are expected rise a solid
0.9%mth, taking annual inflation to 3.5%, while gas & other
household fuels provide the main offset, falling 2.5%mth.
Transport is expected to be the main source of upward
pressure in October, rising 2.9%mth and lifting to 11.6%yr.

This largely reflects an 8.2%mth jump in auto fuel prices
following the rise in global oil prices through the month. Motor
vehicle prices and spare parts are also expected to increase, by
0.4%mth and 1.6%mth respectively.

Recreation & culture is expected to provide the key offset, 
falling 1.5%mth, driven by a 3.0%mth decline in holiday travel. 
We also expect some pull-back across components exposed 
to the AI trade, including audio, visual & computing equipment 
(–0.2%mth) and games, toys & hobbies (–0.3%mth).
Underlying inflation holds momentum
As noted above, our October TM estimate is also 0.4%mth, a step 
up from 0.3% in September taking the annual pace to 3.7%yr from 
3.6%yr and lifting the six month annualised pace to 4.2%yr, from 
4.0%yr, the fastest pace in the short history of the series. Rents 
and dwellings continue to underpin the TM estimates. 
Upside risks to October baseline ...
We view the risks around the October CPI outcome as slightly 
tilted to the upside. Our estimates already allow for a greater 
degree of price resetting in areas such as meals out & takeaway 
and personal and household services, but there is a risk that this 
adjustment proves broader than we have assumed. Increases in 
fuel levy surcharges in early October are another source of upside 
risk, including increases by Woolworths and Australia Post, with 
the latter nearly doubling its surcharge between September and 
October. The key downside risk remains housing, particularly 
new dwelling prices, which have surprised to the downside in 
recent months and could continue to do so as the housing market 
downturn deepens. Trade notifications data also point to some 
further downside risk, with builders on average announcing 
smaller price changes than in prior months.
... but momentum to ease in November ...
We have a preliminary estimate for the November CPI of 
–0.1%mth which will see the annual pace ease back to 4.6%yr. 
Auto fuel moved lower through November to be down –2.1% in 
the month with seasonal falls in garments, health, furniture & 
household appliances and holiday travel offsetting some strength 
in food, housing, communication and financial services. 
Seasonally adjusted the CPI is estimated to lift 0.1% in November, 
highlighting the seasonal weakness in the month, while our TM 
estimate is for a 0.2% lift seeing the annual pace ease a touch to 
3.6%yr while the six month annualised pace drops from 4.2%yr 
to 3.9%yr. This moderation is expected to continue into December 
with a preliminary TM estimate of 0.2%mth/3.6%yr. 
The following page has a table with monthly profiles for the CPI, 
seasonally adjusted CPI and the TM out to July 2027. By July 
2027 we now expect an annual pace of 3.3%yr for the CPI and 
seasonally adjusted CPI while our estimate for the TM is 3.0%yr. 
More significantly, the six month annualised pace of the TM is 
estimated to be down to 2.7%yr by June 2027, well within the 
RBA’s target band. We have also included tables breaking down 
the monthly components of the CPI through the December and 
March quarters. 
... with December quarter now the peak ...
Our December quarter CPI estimate has been revised down 
slightly to 1.2%qtr, a modest 0.05ppt revision, with the annual 
pace lifting from 3.9%yr to 4.6%yr. The seasonally adjusted

CPI gains 1.4% in the December quarter highlighting normally
December is a seasonally soft quarter. Our TM estimate for
Q3 has a small downward revision of 0.05ppt to 0.85% at two
decimal place but this rounds up to our published estimate of
0.9%qtr. For now that suggests downside risk for our TM

estimate as getting the quarter to round to 0.8% is much
easier than getting it to round up to 1.0%. We also note
auto fuel prices falling faster than expected which may not
only result in downside risks to out CPI estimate but also our
TM estimate due to less pass through pressure.
... with inflation moderating through 2027 ...
Westpac’s preliminary March quarter CPI estimate is just 0.4%.
Driving most of this outcome is the 7.7% decline in autofuel

(–0.29ppt) and a 3.8% fall in holiday travel (–0.23ppt) being
offset by rising food (1.0%, 0.18ppt), housing (0.7%, 0.15ppt),
health (2.6%, 0.18ppt) and education (3.5%, 0.17ppt). The
March quarter does not have a lot in the way of net seasonally
with the seasonally adjusted estimate up 0.5%. Our underlying
TM measure of inflation is estimated to rise 0.7% in the March
quarter taking the annual pace down to 3.5% while the six
month annualised pace dips from 3.8%yr to 3.2%yr.

... as a disinflationary impulse kicks in.
We have for some time expected auto fuel prices to peak in
October, 240 cpl for petrol and 282 cpl for diesel, then to
trend lower from there. Our November estimates are currently
sitting on 237 cpl and 279 cpl respectively trending down to
186 cpl for petrol and 196 cpl for diesel by December 2027.
This represents a 12.7% fall in auto fuel through the first half
of 2027 subtracting 0.42ppt off the CPI over the same period

of time. For a detailed description on how we constructed the
auto fuels profile please see: “Persistent energy pressures
have further to run”. At this stage we have not adjusted our
Brent forecast in line with the current correction underway
(see the November Market Outlook for more details why) as
we expect this to be a volatile ride. Should the current correc-
tion continue, auto fuel will represent a larger drag on the CPI
and potentially the Trimmed Mean.

Critical to our expected moderation in core inflation is a step
down in dwelling price inflation on the back of falling estab-
lished home prices. From a 1.0%qtr increase in Q3 2026 we
see dwelling price increasing by 0.4% in Q4 2026, and then
0.5% in Q1 and 0.4%qtr in Q2 before dropping to 0.2%qtr in
Q3 and Q4. This will see dwelling price inflation in the CPI
drop from 5.4%yr at June 2026 to 2.3%yr by June 2027 then

down to 1.3%yr by December 2027.
At least to the end of this year there is still a high degree of
uncertainty about how much businesses will be able to pass
through upstream cost pressures. The latest NAB business sur-
vey continues to highlight robust input price pressure but also

that firms are struggling to pass on those increases.
For businesses, it is almost a now or never opportunity to raise
prices. We expect that through 2027, as energy prices ease
and the domestic economy slows under the weight of higher
interest rates and falling established home prices, households
will be much more resistant to price increases especially if dis-
cretionary household spending softens by more than expected.
That said, these headwinds may be at least partially offset by

the stronger starting point for household incomes, which has
proven more resilient than previously anticipated, and a possi-
ble bounce back in energy prices due to renewed conflict in the
Middle East.
Supply or demand driven inflation?
Inflation has steadily drifted away from the RBA’s target over
recent quarters. While the demand side is the larger driver of
inflation currently, most of the recent pick-up in TM inflation
appears to be from the supply side, and the more detailed

breakdown may understate how much.
There are several ways to split inflation into supply and de-
mand. One is to estimate how much of the rise a standard de-
mand-driven model can explain and attribute the rest to supply
(see here). Run through to Q3 2026, this approach puts most of
the recent pick-up in TM inflation down to supply. Without it,
trimmed mean would have risen from around 3.0%yr to 3.2%yr
over the year to Q3 2026, rather than the 2.9%yr to 3.7%yr

WESTPAC ECONOMICS
rise we actually saw. The supply contribution is moderate,
though, and well below its Covid-era peak. This analysis also
reveals that most of the disinflation through late-2023 and into
early-2025 reflected supply pressures unwinding, while de-
mand-driven inflation remained stickier, persisting above 3%yr.

Another is the San Francisco Fed approach, which the RBA has
also replicated (see here). The attribution is that if prices rise
and quantities fall, it is supply; if both rise, it is demand. We run
it to Q2 2026, as Q3 volume data are not yet available. Like the
first approach, it finds that demand accounts for most of infla-
tion (headline in this case). However, unlike the first approach,
it shows supply contributions easing a little.
A closer look at the categories classified as demand tells a

more nuanced story. Utilities, health and insurance all feature,
and their prices are largely shaped by government policy or are
not the categories that usually come to mind when people talk
about demand-driven inflation, and they are not very sensitive
to interest rates. You can look at electricity as a clear example
where prices in recent history have been set by government
policies (the introduction of, and then removal of, cost of living
rebates as well as policies to decarbonise the economy) or ex-

ternal supply shock (rising gas prices pushing up the marginal
cost of generation).
Categories more commonly thought of discretionary do show
up on the demand side, with recreation & culture (excluding
travel) and meals out & takeaway being the key ones. The
drawback of this sort of decomposition is that it labels cate-
gories where prices and quantities move together as demand,
even when this may reflect the ripple effects of a supply shock
elsewhere, a point our Chief Economist Luci Ellis detailed in a

note in 2024 (see In a world gone bananas, don’t squash the
other fruit). Some of this may be occurring now. In particular,
the analysis is likely not capturing the shift in spending towards
these categories resulting from travel disruptions associated
with the Middle East conflict (travel services itself was classi-
fied as a supply-side contributor in Q2). As travel normalises,
some of the “demand” contribution from recreation & culture
and meals out & takeaway should therefore fade over coming

quarters.
We would also acknowledge the work of Tim Toohey and his
analysis “The RBA has an identity problem”.

Moreover, if we get a cooling of tech investment, it could give room
to other parts of the economy to breathe and step up.

## Document 391: The Commodities Feed_ Copper and gold rebound while oil extends gains

Source PDF: all report pdf/The Commodities Feed_ Copper and gold rebound while oil extends gains.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-27
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Copper and gold continued to recover on Friday as risk appetite
improved, while oil extended its advance amid renewed
concerns over Middle East supply disruptions. Zinc also moved
higher after the planned suspension of production at a
European smelter

Ewa Manthey and Warren Patterson
Monthly US copper imports surged to a 12-year high of more than 200,000
tonnes in July ahead of potential tariﬀs
Energy - Middle East risks lift oil prices
ICE Brent extended gains for a fourth consecutive session on Friday,
trading above $97/bbl and heading for its ﬁrst weekly gain this
month. Prices remain supported by attacks on energy infrastructure
and shipping in the Middle East. The latest disruption involved
Saudi Arabia's 400k b/d Jazan reﬁnery, following earlier attacks on
the Ras Tanura export hub and Red Sea ports, adding uncertainty
around the recovery in regional crude exports.
Attention now turns to Sunday's OPEC+ meeting. The group is
expected to consider keeping current production quotas unchanged

through January, although some members have been pushing for
higher quotas. Meanwhile, Saudi Aramco is expected to cut the
January Arab Light OSP to Asia, after setting December barrels at a
$7/bbl discount to the Oman-Dubai benchmark.
In reﬁned products, inventories moved in opposite directions across
the main trading hubs. ARA stocks fell by 280kt WoW to 4.2mt, led
by lower gasoil and naphtha inventories. Singapore stocks,
meanwhile, increased by 949k barrels to 40.9m barrels, driven by

higher light distillate and residual fuel stocks, while middle
distillates edged lower.
Metals - Copper rebounds as risk appetite
improves
Copper extended its recovery on Friday, with LME prices climbing
back above $12,600/t after falling below $11,500/t earlier in the
week. The rebound came alongside stronger global equities as risk
appetite improved following several volatile sessions.
The move appears to have been ampliﬁed by short covering, while

the physical market in China remains relatively ﬁrm. The Yangshan
premium has eased from last week’s record levels of $500/t but
remains elevated at around $320/t, while the spread between
Chinese spot prices and international benchmarks continues to
point to a premium for metal in China.
Focus will remain on Chinese demand and inventories, as well as
the potential movement of US-held copper back into the global
market.
In other metals, zinc prices jumped, with LME 3-month futures

gaining more than 3% yesterday after Nyrstar announced plans to
suspend zinc production at its Budel smelter in the Netherlands.
The 300ktpa facility has been under strategic review since
September, with high European energy costs continuing to weigh
on smelter margins. The suspension has added to concerns over
reﬁned zinc availability, while highlighting the competitive
pressures facing European smelters as Chinese production expands.

^New 
Transitioning to mid-cycle 
• We now favour a more balanced stance between stocks and bonds. Near-
term event risks and high earnings expectations prompt us to trim equities to a 
Core holding. The US and Asia ex-Japan (AxJ) remain our preferred regions. 
Within AxJ, we upgrade India to Overweight. 
• Higher bond yields are improving the outlook for future returns and offering 
increasingly attractive income opportunities. We are Overweight Emerging 
Market (EM) USD bonds and favour corporate over government bonds, but limit 
duration to 3-7 years. 
• We remain Overweight gold. Prices are likely to grind higher as near-term 
headwinds from higher bond yields are offset by EM central bank demand. 
Great expectations 
Individual risk events, high earnings expectations and a higher cost of capital 
point to a more moderate return environment ahead. In the very near term, 
financial markets must navigate key event risks, such as (i) the US mid-term elections, 
which have historically been associated with elevated market volatility and (ii) renewed 
geopolitical tensions, with several reports suggesting the US is considering renewed 
strikes on Iran. These concerns have pushed the Brent oil price well above USD 
110/bbl despite significant ongoing crude oil flows through the Strait of Hormuz.  
Higher bond yields combined with high earnings expectations raise the risk of 
an expectations reset. The US Q3 earnings season is attracting significant attention 
given high earnings expectations, particularly for the AI sector. However, higher US 
bond yields may start to bite as debt-financed capex takes on a greater share of total 
capex over the coming year. This, combined with already-high expectations, raises the 
risk of disappointment and a near-term repricing of expected earnings growth pace.  
Despite the current challenges, healthy growth continues to underpin the 
economic backdrop. The US economic outlook remains well supported, and the 
absolute level of year-ahead earnings expectations is high. We do not expect Fed rate 
hikes to result in overly restrictive policy. Our caution, thus, stems from the risk of an 
expectations repricing rather than an outright economic slowdown.

1 Investment strategy and key themes

Steve Brice 
Global Chief Investment Officer 
Manpreet Gill

Fig. 1 Our quant model score has fallen to levels 
associated with flat-to-modestly-negative equity returns  
 Fig. 2 Negative gold-USD correlation has held back

A more balanced stock-bond mix 
We trim global equities to a Core holding, leaving us 
Neutral on both equities and bonds and Overweight gold. 
The concerns laid out earlier point to near-term volatility risks 
and a somewhat lower long-term growth trajectory for 
equities. Our quant model also reflects this cautious outlook, 
having downgraded equities to a level usually associated with 
flat-to-modestly-negative returns. 
From a relative perspective, though, the higher yields on 
offer from bonds also raise the bar by presenting an 
increasingly compelling return outlook after several 
years of very low yields. We believe this argues for portfolios 
that are increasingly balanced between equities and bonds. 
Ways to diversify equity exposure 
We continue to favour global equity market 
diversification, with a preference for US and AxJ equities. 
We remain Overweight US and AxJ equities, expecting them 
to outperform other major regions. Earnings growth remains 
the main driver of this view – while growth rates could 
decelerate somewhat, we still expect them to remain higher 
than those of other major regions.  
Japan and Euro area equities remain attractive as 
regional diversifiers. Our positive view on both markets’ 
financial sectors supports our Core holding view on them.   
Within Asia, we are Overweight Taiwan and India. Indian 
equities have lagged the region this year, in part due to limited 
listed AI-related exposure. However, we believe this is 
increasingly likely to become an advantage as investors seek 
greater diversification away from excessive concentration in 
the AI theme. This comes at a time when the economic and 
earnings growth outlook for Indian equities is starting to 
improve. We retain a more Neutral view on Chinese equities 
amid a still-challenging growth outlook. 
We also favour greater sector diversification. In the US, 
we favour diversifying across our Overweight sectors of 
technology, financials, materials and healthcare. We also 
favour financials in Japan and the Euro area.  
Changes to our equity opportunistic ideas also reflect a 
more defensive stance. In recent weeks, we have closed our 
global semiconductors, MSCI Taiwan, US communication 
services and global power & electrification ideas and have 
opened new ideas on US healthcare and MSCI Switzerland. 
Riding the yield train 
We believe bond markets offer an attractive opportunity 
to lock in yields. The 10-year US government bond yield is 
now in a range last witnessed in 2007. While the process of 
rising yields results in lower bond prices – as painfully 
experienced in recent months – the yield level itself starts to 
significantly improve the outlook for future returns. 
Favour corporate over government bonds, but check 
your duration. Still-high credit quality means that we continue 
to favour taking on credit risk to earn a higher yield (EM USD 
bonds are preferred, but we also see investment-grade (IG) 
and high-yield (HY) bonds as Core holdings). However, 
limiting duration to 3-7 years remains key to avoiding 
excessive exposure to any inflation surprises. 
Changes to our bond opportunistic ideas also reflect a 
more balanced stance. We close our EU bank contingent 
convertible bonds (CoCos2 – please refer page 27) and US 
utilities corporate hybrids ideas. We also close our 
opportunistic idea on US 10-year government bonds following 
the recent pullback in yields, locking in gains. 
Alternatives and gold to remain relevant 
Alternatives are an effective tool for portfolio risk 
management. Hedge funds and private markets provide a 
differentiated return stream, while rotating into alternatives 
helps reduce equity beta exposure. We examine downside 
capture and how beta is reduced via hedge fund sub-
strategies and private markets in our asset allocation section.  
Gold remains a key Overweight. We trim our three- and 12-
month forecasts modestly, but see gold forming a strong base 
above USD 4,000/oz. Rising bond yields and the USD were 
recent headwinds, but we expect a softer USD and continued 
EM central bank demand to result in 12-month gains.

### Page 7

Global Market Outlook 7

Key themes

Core scenario (soft landing, 45% probability): An economic soft landing remains our core scenario, with the global economy 
overcoming this year’s oil price shock thanks to a surge in AI investment in the US and Asia. Record wealth generation driven 
by the stock market rally is supporting US consumption. Fiscal easing in the US, Germany and Japan is supporting growth. 
Meanwhile, inflation has reaccelerated due to a rebound in fuel prices as the Middle East conflict drags on. Against this backdrop, 
the Fed, ECB and BoJ all hiked rates in the same month (in September) for the first time. Although all three subsequently paused 
in October, we expect the Fed to hike twice more, the ECB once more and the BoJ thrice more by June 2027. Nevertheless, we 
expect the energy and tariff effects on inflation to fade by H2 2027, enabling the Fed and the ECB to cut rates by Q3 2027. 
Meanwhile, China is likely to target credit easing and accelerate fiscal spending in H1 2027 to revive domestic growth.  
Upside risk (no landing, 30% probability): We maintain the probability at 30% as AI-driven investment growth continues. 
There is a reasonable chance that lower oil prices next year, the AI-fuelled wealth boom and fiscal easing in Germany and Japan 
will boost ‘animal spirits’. A potential global defence spending boom, led by the US and Europe, could also lift global growth. 
Downside risk (25% probability): We raised the risk of a recession to 15%, especially if central banks are forced to accelerate 
the pace of rate hikes to douse rising inflation. A stock market downturn negating the wealth effect or a bond sell-off due to fiscal 
concerns are other tail risks. We cut stagflation probability to 10% as we see low risk of oil prices sustaining above USD 120/bbl.

Key chart  
The US-led AI-focused 
investment boom has lifted 
business confidence. The 
resulting equity market 
rally has boosted US 
household wealth, 
supporting consumption 
 Fig. 8 Robust business confidence amid AI boom; equity rally lifts US household net worth 
Purchasing managers indices; US household net worth as percentage of personal disposable income

Source: Bloomberg, Standard Chartered

Policy rates watch  
We expect the Fed to hike twice more by Q2 2027, then 
ease in H2 2027, as AI-led expansion continues. The US 
economy remains firmly in expansion. In Q3, annualised GDP 
growth accelerated to 3.4%, led by AI investments and 
consumption. In October, the composite Purchasing 
Managers’ Index (PMI) remained strong at 57.6. Since our last 
monthly report, the consensus estimate for 2026 GDP growth 
has been nudged up to 2.3% from 2.1%, and the 2027 
estimate has risen to 2.2%. That matches the Fed’s upgraded 
growth projection of 2.3% for 2026, but remains below the 
Fed’s 2.4% estimate for 2027. AI investment, fiscal support 
and household wealth are the main growth drivers, although 
consumer activity lags business spending. Higher bond yields 
and elevated oil prices are headwinds, but recession risks 
look contained. Our core scenario remains a ‘soft landing’, at 
45%, with a meaningful 30% chance of a ‘no-landing’ upside. 
Rising gasoline and diesel prices pushed US core personal 
consumption expenditures (PCE) inflation to 0.3% m/m and 
3.1% y/y, matching estimates. Consensus sees core PCE 
inflation slowing from 3.2% in Q4 2026 to 2.4% by Q4 2027 
as energy and tariff effects fade. Underlying price momentum 
is softening, and recent PCE methodology revisions have 
lowered core inflation. However, fuel costs passed on to wider 
consumers and AI-driven chip shortages remain upside risks. 
In October, the Fed held rates at 4.0%, after hiking in 
September for the first time since 2023. We expect the Fed to 
hike rates by 25bps in December and 25bps more in H1 2027 
due to elevated near-term energy inflation. It is likely to follow 
with a 25bps cut to 4.25% in Q3 2027 as disinflation returns. 
This aligns with the Fed’s projected policy path, although it 
implies a more dovish trajectory than money markets, which 
are pricing another 56bps of hikes over the next 12 months. 
Political uncertainty after the mid-term election is a key risk.
